</code></pre></div></div><div data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">The <code>id</code> must be unique across all extensions and will be used to fetch values.</span>
<span data-as="p">If you do not <a href="#categories" class="link">provide a category</a>, then the <code>id</code> will be split by
<code>.</code> to determine where it appears in the settings panel.</span>
<ul>
<li>If your <code>id</code> doesn’t contain any <code>.</code> then it will appear in the “Other”
category and your <code>id</code> will be used as the section heading.</li>
<li>If your <code>id</code> contains at least one <code>.</code> then the leftmost part will be used
as the setting category and the second part will be used as the section
heading. Any further parts are ignored.</li>
</ul>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">    console</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.</span><span style="color:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">log</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;Setting is disabled.&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div><div data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="react-to-changes"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#react-to-changes" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">React to changes</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The <code>onChange()</code> event handler will be called as soon as the user changes the
setting in the settings panel.</span>
<span data-as="p">This will also be called when the extension is registered, on every page load.</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="slider"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#slider" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Slider</span></h3>

are not supported.</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="combo"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#combo" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Combo</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">This lets the user pick from a drop-down list of values.</span>
<span data-as="p">You can provide options either as a plain string or as an object with <code>text</code>
and <code>value</code> fields. If you only provide a plain string, then it will be used
for both.</span>
<span data-as="p">You can let the user enter freeform text by supplying the <code>editable: true</code>
attribute, or search by supplying the <code>filter: true</code> attribute.</span>
<span data-as="p">Based on the <a href="https://primevue.org/select/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">Select PrimeVue component</a>. Groups
are not supported.</span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">        { </span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">text:</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;My first option&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">value:</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;first&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">        &quot;My second option&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    ],</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#C8C8C8">    attrs</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#C8C8C8">        editable</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">true</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#C8C8C8">        filter</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: </span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">true</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    },</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#C8C8C8">    onChange</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">: (</span><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">newVal</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, </span><span style="color:#953800;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">oldVal</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">) </span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">=&gt;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">        console</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.</span><span style="color:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">log</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">`Setting was changed from </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">${</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">oldVal</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">}</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> to </span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">${</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">newVal</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">}</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">`</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">    },</span></span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="color"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#color" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Color</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">This lets the user select a color from a color picker or type in a hex
reference.</span>
<span data-as="p">Note that the format requires six full hex digits - three digit shorthand does
not work.</span>
<span data-as="p">Based on the <a href="https://primevue.org/colorpicker/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" class="link">ColorPicker PrimeVue
component</a>.</span>

<span data-as="p">Hidden settings aren’t displayed in the settings panel, but you can read and
write to them from your code.</span>

<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="categories"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#categories" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Categories</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">You can specify the categorisation of your setting separately to the <code>id</code>.
This means you can change the categorisation and naming without changing the
<code>id</code> and losing the values that have already been set by users.</span>
